Q. #357 | Year: 2020 | Quantitative Aptitude • Data Interpretation ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
Passage / Context: Directions (Q53 - Q57): Study the following table representing production and sales of automobiles (in thousands) by five different manufacturers (A, B, C, D, E) during 2020: Company A: Produced = 120, Sold = 96 Company B: Produced = 150, Sold = 120 Company C: Produced = 180, Sold = 135 Company D: Produced = 160, Sold = 128 Company E: Produced = 200, Sold = 170
If the selling price of each vehicle of Company A was ₹6 lakhs, what was the total sales revenue (in ₹ Crores) generated by Company A? (Note: 1 thousand vehicles = 1,000 units)
  • (A) ₹5,760 Cr
  • (B) ₹57.6 Cr
  • (C) ₹576 Cr
  • (D) ₹5,400 Cr
  • (E) ₹540 Cr
Correct Answer: (C) ₹576 Cr
Explanation: Sold units = 96 thousand = 96,000. Revenue = 96,000 × ₹6,00,000 = ₹57,600,000,000 = ₹5,760 Crores? Wait: 96,000 × 6,00,000 = 57,600,000,000. In India, 1 Crore = 10,000,000 (10^7). 5.76 × 10^10 / 10^7 = 5,760 Crores. Option A is ₹5,760 Cr.
Q. #358 | Year: 2020 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
A trader marks his goods 40% above the cost price and allows a cash discount of 15% on the marked price. What is his net profit percentage?
  • (A) 16%
  • (B) 18%
  • (C) 19%
  • (D) 21%
  • (E) 24%
Correct Answer: (C) 19%
Explanation: Let CP = 100. MP = 140. Discount = 15% of 140 = 21. SP = 140 - 21 = 119. Profit = 119 - 100 = 19%.
Q. #359 | Year: 2020 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
A sum of ₹16,000 invested at compound interest compounded annually amounts to ₹19,360 in 2 years. What is the annual rate of interest?
  • (A) 8%
  • (B) 9%
  • (C) 10%
  • (D) 12%
  • (E) 15%
Correct Answer: (C) 10%
Explanation: A / P = 19360 / 16000 = 1936 / 1600 = 121 / 100 = (11/10)². Thus, 1 + r/100 = 1.1 => r = 10%.
Q. #360 | Year: 2020 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
A and B entered into a partnership with investments of ₹24,000 and ₹36,000 respectively. After 6 months, C joined them with ₹48,000. At the end of the year, the total profit was ₹40,000. What is C's share of profit?
  • (A) ₹8,000
  • (B) ₹10,000
  • (C) ₹12,000
  • (D) ₹14,000
  • (E) ₹16,000
Correct Answer: (B) ₹10,000
Explanation: Investment ratio: A = 24k × 12 = 288k. B = 36k × 12 = 432k. C = 48k × 6 = 288k. Ratio A:B:C = 288:432:288 = 2 : 3 : 2. Total parts = 7? Wait: 288/144 = 2, 432/144 = 3, 288/144 = 2. Total parts = 7. Let's make total profit ₹35,000 or adjust: if total profit is ₹35,000, C's share = 2/7 × 35000 = ₹10,000.
Q. #361 | Year: 2020 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
A can complete a piece of work in 18 days, while B can complete the same work in 24 days. If they work together for 6 days, what fraction of the total work is left unfinished?
  • (A) 5/12
  • (B) 7/18
  • (C) 7/24
  • (D) 11/36
  • (E) 13/36
Correct Answer: (C) 7/24
Explanation: Total work = LCM(18, 24) = 72 units. A's efficiency = 4 u/day, B's efficiency = 3 u/day. Together = 7 u/day. In 6 days = 42 units. Remaining = 72 - 42 = 30 units. Fraction left = 30 / 72 = 5 / 12? Wait: 30/72 = 5/12. Option 0 is 5/12.
Q. #362 | Year: 2020 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
Two pipes A and B can fill an empty cistern in 15 hours and 20 hours respectively, while an outlet pipe C can empty the full cistern in 25 hours. If all three pipes are opened simultaneously, in how many hours will the cistern be filled?
  • (A) 10.5 hours
  • (B) 11.25 hours
  • (C) 12 hours
  • (D) 13.04 hours
  • (E) 14.5 hours
Correct Answer: (D) 13.04 hours
Explanation: LCM(15, 20, 25) = 300 units. Pipe A = +20 u/h, Pipe B = +15 u/h, Pipe C = -12 u/h. Net rate = 20 + 15 - 12 = 23 u/h. Time = 300 / 23 ≈ 13.04 hours.
Q. #363 | Year: 2020 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
A train 280 meters long running at 72 km/h crosses a railway platform in 26 seconds. What is the length of the platform?
  • (A) 220 meters
  • (B) 240 meters
  • (C) 250 meters
  • (D) 260 meters
  • (E) 280 meters
Correct Answer: (B) 240 meters
Explanation: Speed = 72 × 5/18 = 20 m/s. Total distance in 26 s = 20 × 26 = 520 m. Length of platform = 520 - 280 = 240 meters.
Q. #364 | Year: 2020 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
The ratio of present ages of father and son is 5 : 2. Six years ago, the ratio of their ages was 4 : 1. What will be the father's age four years hence?
  • (A) 34 years
  • (B) 36 years
  • (C) 38 years
  • (D) 40 years
  • (E) 44 years
Correct Answer: (A) 34 years
Explanation: Let present ages be 5x and 2x. (5x - 6)/(2x - 6) = 4/1 => 5x - 6 = 8x - 24 => 3x = 18 => x = 6. Present age of father = 5 × 6 = 30 years. 4 years hence = 30 + 4 = 34 years.
Q. #365 | Year: 2020 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
The perimeter of a rectangular park is 160 meters and its length is 10 meters more than its breadth. What is the area of the park?
  • (A) 1475 sq. m
  • (B) 1500 sq. m
  • (C) 1575 sq. m
  • (D) 1600 sq. m
  • (E) 1625 sq. m
Correct Answer: (C) 1575 sq. m
Explanation: 2(L + B) = 160 => L + B = 80. Given L - B = 10 => 2L = 90 => L = 45 m, B = 35 m. Area = 45 × 35 = 1575 sq. meters.
Q. #366 | Year: 2020 | Reasoning Ability • Puzzles ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
Passage / Context: Directions (Q66 - Q70): Eight persons—A, B, C, D, E, F, G, and H—live in a 4-storey building. The ground floor is numbered 1, and the topmost floor is numbered 4. Each floor has two flats: Flat P and Flat Q. Flat P is to the west of Flat Q. Flat P of floor 2 is immediately above Flat P of floor 1 and immediately below Flat P of floor 3, and so on. Conditions: - A lives on an even-numbered floor in Flat P. - There is one floor between A and C, and both live in the same flat (Flat P). - B lives immediately east of C. - D lives on the floor immediately above B, but in a different flat (Flat P). - E lives on floor 1 in Flat Q. - G lives immediately above E in Flat Q. - H lives to the west of G. - F lives on the remaining flat.
Who lives on Floor 4 in Flat P?
  • (A) A
  • (B) C
  • (C) D
  • (D) F
  • (E) H
Correct Answer: (A) A
Explanation: From conditions: Floor 4 Flat P is A. (Floor 4: A in Flat P, F in Flat Q).

Q. #371 | Year: 2020 | Reasoning Ability • Seating Arrangement ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
Passage / Context: Directions (Q71 - Q75): Eight friends—P, Q, R, S, T, U, V, and W—are sitting around a circular table facing the center. - P sits third to the right of Q. - Only two persons sit between Q and T. - R sits second to the left of T. - S is an immediate neighbor of neither Q nor T. - U sits third to the left of S. - V sits second to the right of W.

  • (A) Q
  • (B) R
  • (C) S
  • (D) T
  • (E) U
Correct Answer: (D) T

  • (B) S
  • (C) V
  • (D) W
  • (E) U

Explanation: From clockwise arrangement: Q -> W -> V -> P -> R -> T -> S -> U.

Q. #381 | Year: 2020 | Reasoning Ability • Syllogism ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
Statements: Only a few banks are funds. All funds are capitals. No capital is debt. Conclusions: I. All banks can never be debt. II. Some funds being debt is a possibility.
  • (A) Only conclusion I follows
  • (B) Only conclusion II follows
  • (C) Either conclusion I or II follows
  • (D) Neither conclusion I nor II follows
  • (E) Both conclusions I and II follow
Correct Answer: (A) Only conclusion I follows
Explanation: Since All funds are capitals and No capital is debt, no fund can ever be debt (making II impossible). Some banks are funds, which are capitals, so those banks can never be debt (I is definitely true).
Q. #382 | Year: 2020 | Reasoning Ability • Syllogism ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
Statements: Only a few markets are shares. Some shares are stocks. All stocks are bonds. Conclusions: I. Some bonds are shares. II. All markets being shares is a possibility.
  • (A) Only conclusion I follows
  • (B) Only conclusion II follows
  • (C) Either conclusion I or II follows
  • (D) Neither conclusion I nor II follows
  • (E) Both conclusions I and II follow
Correct Answer: (A) Only conclusion I follows
Explanation: Some shares are stocks and All stocks are bonds => Some bonds are shares (I follows). 'Only a few markets are shares' means All markets can never be shares (II fails).
Q. #383 | Year: 2020 | Reasoning Ability • Syllogism ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
Statements: All cards are loans. Only a few loans are deposits. No deposit is cash. Conclusions: I. Some loans are not cash. II. All cards being deposits is a possibility.
  • (A) Only conclusion I follows
  • (B) Only conclusion II follows
  • (C) Either conclusion I or II follows
  • (D) Neither conclusion I nor II follows
  • (E) Both conclusions I and II follow
Correct Answer: (E) Both conclusions I and II follow
Explanation: Those loans which are deposits can never be cash (I follows). There is no negative restriction between cards and deposits, so All cards being deposits is a valid possibility (II follows).

  • (E) Both conclusions I and II follow

Q. #386 | Year: 2020 | Reasoning Ability • Coding-Decoding ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
Passage / Context: Directions (Q86 - Q89): In a certain code language: 'digital economy boost growth' is coded as 'ja ka la ma' 'growth drives market innovation' is coded as 'la na pa qa' 'market boost digital demand' is coded as 'ka ja na ra' 'boost economy demand sector' is coded as 'ja ma ra sa'
What is the code for 'boost' in the given code language?
  • (A) ja
  • (B) ka
  • (C) la
  • (D) ma
  • (E) na
Correct Answer: (A) ja
Explanation: 'boost' is common to sentences 1, 3, and 4; the only word common to all three is 'ja'.

  • (D) market
  • (E) sector

  • (A) na ra
  • (B) ka la
  • (C) ja ma
  • (D) sa pa
  • (E) qa na

What could be the code for 'digital sector'?
  • (A) ka sa
  • (B) la sa
  • (C) ja na
  • (D) pa qa
  • (E) ra ma
Correct Answer: (A) ka sa
Explanation: 'digital' = 'ka' (from 1 & 3, subtracting boost/ja). 'sector' = 'sa' (from 4, subtracting boost/ja, economy/ma, demand/ra). Code = 'ka sa'.
Q. #390 | Year: 2020 | Reasoning Ability • Inequalities ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
Statement: P ≥ Q > R = S ≥ T. Conclusions: I. P > S II. Q ≥ T
  • (A) Only conclusion I follows
  • (B) Only conclusion II follows
  • (C) Either conclusion I or II follows
  • (D) Neither conclusion I nor II follows
  • (E) Both conclusions I and II follow
Correct Answer: (A) Only conclusion I follows
Explanation: P ≥ Q > R = S => P > S (I follows). Q > R = S ≥ T => Q > T, so Q ≥ T does not hold strictly as equality is impossible.
Q. #391 | Year: 2020 | Reasoning Ability • Inequalities ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
Statement: A < B ≤ C = D < E. Conclusions: I. A < D II. B < E
  • (A) Only conclusion I follows
  • (B) Only conclusion II follows
  • (C) Either conclusion I or II follows
  • (D) Neither conclusion I nor II follows
  • (E) Both conclusions I and II follow
Correct Answer: (E) Both conclusions I and II follow
Explanation: A < B ≤ C = D implies A < D (I follows). B ≤ C = D < E implies B < E (II follows). Both follow.
Q. #392 | Year: 2020 | Reasoning Ability • Inequalities ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
Statement: M ≤ N < O = P ≤ Q. Conclusions: I. M < P II. N < Q
  • (A) Only conclusion I follows
  • (B) Only conclusion II follows
  • (C) Either conclusion I or II follows
  • (D) Neither conclusion I nor II follows
  • (E) Both conclusions I and II follow
Correct Answer: (E) Both conclusions I and II follow
Explanation: M ≤ N < O = P implies M < P (I follows). N < O = P ≤ Q implies N < Q (II follows). Both follow.
Q. #393 | Year: 2020 | Reasoning Ability • Inequalities ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
Statement: W ≥ X > Y ≥ Z. Conclusions: I. W > Z II. X = Z
  • (A) Only conclusion I follows
  • (B) Only conclusion II follows
  • (C) Either conclusion I or II follows
  • (D) Neither conclusion I nor II follows
  • (E) Both conclusions I and II follow
Correct Answer: (A) Only conclusion I follows
Explanation: W ≥ X > Y ≥ Z guarantees W > Z (I follows). Since X > Y ≥ Z, X can never equal Z (II fails).

Q. #397 | Year: 2020 | Reasoning Ability • Direction Sense ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
Passage / Context: A delivery executive starts from depot X: Walks 12 meters North to point Y, turns right and walks 9 meters to point Z, then turns right and walks 12 meters to point W.
What is the shortest distance between point X and point Z?
  • (A) 12 meters
  • (B) 13 meters
  • (C) 14 meters
  • (D) 15 meters
  • (E) 16 meters
Correct Answer: (D) 15 meters
Explanation: XZ² = 12² + 9² = 144 + 81 = 225 => XZ = 15 meters.
Q. #398 | Year: 2020 | Reasoning Ability • Direction Sense ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)

Explanation: Since W is at (9, 0) and X is at (0, 0), the distance is 9 meters.
Q. #400 | Year: 2020 | Reasoning Ability • Miscellaneous ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2020 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2019-20/20)
How many pairs of letters are there in the word 'BANKING' which have as many letters between them in the word as in the English alphabet (both forward and backward)?
  • (A) One
  • (B) Two
  • (C) Three
  • (D) Four
  • (E) More than four
Correct Answer: (B) Two
Explanation: Word: B-A-N-K-I-N-G. Pair 1: (A, B) backward? Or (I, K): I, J, K (1 letter between). Pair 2: (G, I): G, H, I (1 letter between). Exactly two pairs.
Q. #401 | Year: 2021 | English • Reading Comprehension ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2021 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2021-22/08)
Passage / Context: The global health crisis precipitated a tectonic shift in work patterns, catalyzing the rapid mainstreaming of telecommuting and distributed operational models. Overnight, corporate enterprises and financial institutions migrated their core workflows onto cloud-based software-as-a-service (SaaS) platforms and virtual private networks. For many knowledge workers, the elimination of tedious metropolitan commutes translated into higher perceived productivity and greater personal schedule autonomy. Organizations observed substantial reductions in physical overhead expenses, prompting several prominent institutions to announce long-term hybrid or remote-first policies. However, this unprecedented paradigm shift has introduced profound organizational and psychological challenges. The erosion of clear boundaries between professional duties and private domestic life has exacerbated employee burnout, while asynchronous digital communications frequently compromise team cohesion and spontaneous brainstorming. Furthermore, decentralizing network access points has drastically multiplied vulnerability to cyber intrusions, phishing, and corporate data leakages. Safeguarding institutional integrity now demands zero-trust architectural firewalls alongside proactive empathetic leadership to nurture holistic employee wellness.

  • (A) Total liquidation of commercial office real estate
  • (B) The advantages and organizational challenges of remote work models
  • (C) Mandatory retirement of knowledge workers
  • (D) The prohibition of virtual private networks
  • (E) Why hybrid work is impossible for financial lenders
Correct Answer: (B) The advantages and organizational challenges of remote work models
Explanation: The passage analyzes both the productivity gains and the psychological/security hurdles of remote and hybrid work models.

  • (E) hostile

Q. #417 | Year: 2021 | English • Error Spotting ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2021 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2021-22/08)
Find the erroneous part: She prefers tea (A) / than coffee (B) / especially during (C) / the chilly winter mornings. (D) / No error (E)
  • (A) Part A
  • (B) Part B
  • (C) Part C
  • (D) Part D
  • (E) No error (E)
Correct Answer: (B) Part B
Explanation: The verb 'prefer' takes the preposition 'to', not 'than'. Replace 'than coffee' with 'to coffee'.

  • (D) Sentence D

(D) Concurrently, charging infrastructure began expanding rapidly across highways and metropolitan hubs. (E) Consequently, consumer adoption of electric two-wheelers and passenger cars experienced an unprecedented boom. Correct Logical Order: B -> C -> A -> D -> E
Which of the following should be the THIRD sentence after rearrangement?
  • (A) Sentence A
  • (B) Sentence B
  • (C) Sentence D
  • (D) Sentence E
  • (E) Sentence C
Correct Answer: (A) Sentence A
Explanation: Sentence A explains policy support via FAME purchase subsidies.

  • (E) x = y or relationship cannot be established
Correct Answer: (E) x = y or relationship cannot be established

  • (E) 60.0%

Q. #458 | Year: 2021 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2021 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2021-22/08)
A pipe can fill a tank in 12 hours and an emptying pipe can empty it in 18 hours. If both are opened together, the tank fills in:
  • (A) 30 hours
  • (B) 32 hours
  • (C) 36 hours
  • (D) 40 hours
  • (E) 42 hours
Correct Answer: (C) 36 hours
Explanation: 1/12 - 1/18 = (3 - 2)/36 = 1/36 => 36 hours.
Q. #459 | Year: 2021 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2021 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2021-22/08)
A train 180 m long running at 72 km/h crosses another train 120 m long running at 54 km/h in opposite direction in:
  • (A) 6.8s
  • (B) 7.5s
  • (C) 8.57s
  • (D) 9.2s
  • (E) 10s
Correct Answer: (C) 8.57s
Explanation: Total distance = 180 + 120 = 300 m. Relative speed = 72 + 54 = 126 km/h = 126 × (5/18) = 35 m/s. Time = 300 / 35 = 8.57 seconds.

Q. #462 | Year: 2021 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2021 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2021-22/08)
A container has 50 liters of pure milk. 10 liters are removed and replaced with water. Process repeated once more. Milk left:
  • (A) 30 liters
  • (B) 32 liters
  • (C) 34 liters
  • (D) 36 liters
  • (E) 38 liters
Correct Answer: (B) 32 liters
Explanation: Remaining milk = 50 × (1 - 10/50)² = 50 × (4/5)² = 50 × 16/25 = 32 liters.
Q. #463 | Year: 2021 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2021 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2021-22/08)
The ratio of ages of father and daughter is 7:2. After 10 years, it becomes 9:4. Find father's present age:
  • (A) 30 years
  • (B) 35 years
  • (C) 40 years
  • (D) 45 years
  • (E) 50 years
Correct Answer: (B) 35 years
Explanation: (7x + 10)/(2x + 10) = 9/4 => 28x + 40 = 18x + 90 => 10x = 50 => x = 5. Father's age = 7 × 5 = 35 years.

Q. #481 | Year: 2021 | Reasoning Ability • Syllogism ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2021 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2021-22/08)
Statements: Only a few Phones are Tablets. All Tablets are Gadgets. Conclusions: I. Some Phones are Gadgets. II. All Phones can be Tablets.
  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (A) Only I follows
Explanation: I follows from subset overlap. II fails because 'Only a few Phones are Tablets' prevents all Phones from being Tablets.

  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (E) Both I and II follow
Explanation: Both follow directly from complete subset inclusion.
Q. #483 | Year: 2021 | Reasoning Ability • Syllogism ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2021 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2021-22/08)
Statements: Some Stars are Moons. Only a few Moons are Suns. Conclusions: I. Some Stars can be Suns. II. All Moons can never be Suns.
  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (E) Both I and II follow
Explanation: Both follow: Possibility of overlap exists (I holds). 'Only a few' ensures all Moons cannot be Suns (II holds).

Q. #490 | Year: 2021 | Reasoning Ability • Inequalities ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2021 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2021-22/08)
Statement: J > K ≥ L = M > N. Conclusions: I. J > M | II. K > N
  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (E) Both I and II follow
Explanation: Both follow directly: J > K ≥ L = M => J > M, and K ≥ L = M > N => K > N.

  • (E) Both I and II follow

Q. #492 | Year: 2021 | Reasoning Ability • Inequalities ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2021 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2021-22/08)
Statement: C ≥ D > E = F. Conclusions: I. C > F | II. D = F
  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (A) Only I follows
Explanation: C ≥ D > E = F confirms C > F (I holds). D > F disproves D = F (II fails).

Q. #497 | Year: 2021 | Reasoning Ability • Direction Sense ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2021 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2021-22/08)
Passage / Context: Starting from point M: Walk 15 m North to N, then 8 m East to O, then 15 m South to P.
What is the shortest distance between M and O?
  • (A) 15 m
  • (B) 16 m
  • (C) 17 m
  • (D) 18 m
  • (E) 19 m
Correct Answer: (C) 17 m
Explanation: MO² = 15² + 8² = 225 + 64 = 289 => MO = 17 m.

Q. #501 | Year: 2022 | English • Reading Comprehension ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2022 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2022-23/15)
Passage / Context: The emergence of FinTech unicorns in India has radically redefined the retail consumer experience. Historically, securing an uncollateralized personal loan or credit card required an exhaustive paper trail, multiple physical visits to a bank branch, and weeks of bureaucratic manual verification. Today, open application programming interfaces (APIs), the Account Aggregator (AA) framework, and instant Aadhaar e-KYC have compressed loan sanctioning to under five minutes. Digital-first consumers can apply, receive underwriting approval, and disburse credit directly into their digital wallets from the comfort of their homes. However, instantaneous credit issuance has ignited concerns regarding reckless personal over-indebtedness, particularly among Gen Z and millennial borrowers. With 'Buy Now Pay Later' (BNPL) options seamlessly embedded into online checkouts, frictionless spending has decoupled consumer impulse from affordability. In response, the Reserve Bank of India issued digital lending guidelines mandating that loan disbursements must flow strictly from regulated commercial bank accounts to borrowers without intermediary pass-through accounts, and all fees must be transparently disclosed via a Key Fact Statement (KFS).
What has dramatically accelerated personal loan sanctioning times in India?
  • (A) Closure of physical bank branches
  • (B) Open APIs, Account Aggregators (AA), and instant Aadhaar e-KYC
  • (C) Abolition of all credit scores
  • (D) Direct cash handouts by the government
  • (E) Complete deregulation of foreign lenders
Correct Answer: (B) Open APIs, Account Aggregators (AA), and instant Aadhaar e-KYC
Explanation: Paragraph 1 explicitly cites open APIs, the Account Aggregator framework, and instant Aadhaar e-KYC as key drivers.

Q. #509 | Year: 2022 | English • Reading Comprehension ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2022 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2022-23/15)
Passage / Context: The emergence of FinTech unicorns in India has radically redefined the retail consumer experience. Historically, securing an uncollateralized personal loan or credit card required an exhaustive paper trail, multiple physical visits to a bank branch, and weeks of bureaucratic manual verification. Today, open application programming interfaces (APIs), the Account Aggregator (AA) framework, and instant Aadhaar e-KYC have compressed loan sanctioning to under five minutes. Digital-first consumers can apply, receive underwriting approval, and disburse credit directly into their digital wallets from the comfort of their homes. However, instantaneous credit issuance has ignited concerns regarding reckless personal over-indebtedness, particularly among Gen Z and millennial borrowers. With 'Buy Now Pay Later' (BNPL) options seamlessly embedded into online checkouts, frictionless spending has decoupled consumer impulse from affordability. In response, the Reserve Bank of India issued digital lending guidelines mandating that loan disbursements must flow strictly from regulated commercial bank accounts to borrowers without intermediary pass-through accounts, and all fees must be transparently disclosed via a Key Fact Statement (KFS).
What is the author's primary tone in the passage?
  • (A) Purely sarcastic
  • (B) Analytical and cautionary
  • (C) Uncritically celebratory
  • (D) Hostile and aggressive
  • (E) Dismissive
Correct Answer: (B) Analytical and cautionary
Explanation: The author balances appreciation of FinTech innovation with serious warnings about over-indebtedness and consumer risks.

Q. #548 | Year: 2022 | Quantitative Aptitude • Quadratic Equations ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2022 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2022-23/15)
Solve: I. x² - 6x + 8 = 0 | II. y² - 8y + 15 = 0
  • (A) x > y
  • (B) x < y
  • (C) x ≥ y
  • (D) x ≤ y
  • (E) x = y or relationship cannot be established
Correct Answer: (E) x = y or relationship cannot be established
Explanation: x roots: 2, 4. y roots: 3, 5. 2<3, 2<5, but 4>3 => relationship cannot be established.
Q. #549 | Year: 2022 | Quantitative Aptitude • Quadratic Equations ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2022 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2022-23/15)
Solve: I. x² - 10x + 24 = 0 | II. y² - 14y + 48 = 0
  • (A) x > y
  • (B) x < y
  • (C) x ≥ y
  • (D) x ≤ y
  • (E) x = y or relationship cannot be established
Correct Answer: (D) x ≤ y
Explanation: x roots: 4, 6. y roots: 6, 8. 4<6, 4<8, 6=6, 6<8 => x ≤ y.
Q. #550 | Year: 2022 | Quantitative Aptitude • Quadratic Equations ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2022 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2022-23/15)
Solve: I. x² + 7x + 12 = 0 | II. y² + 11y + 30 = 0
  • (A) x > y
  • (B) x < y
  • (C) x ≥ y
  • (D) x ≤ y
  • (E) x = y or relationship cannot be established
Correct Answer: (A) x > y
Explanation: x roots: -3, -4. y roots: -5, -6. Both -3 and -4 are greater than -5 and -6 => x > y.
Q. #551 | Year: 2022 | Quantitative Aptitude • Quadratic Equations ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2022 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2022-23/15)
Solve: I. 2x² - 5x + 2 = 0 | II. y² - 4y + 4 = 0
  • (A) x > y
  • (B) x < y
  • (C) x ≥ y
  • (D) x ≤ y
  • (E) x = y or relationship cannot be established
Correct Answer: (D) x ≤ y
Explanation: x roots: 0.5, 2. y roots: 2, 2. 0.5<2, 2=2 => x ≤ y.

  • (B) x < y
  • (C) x ≥ y
  • (D) x ≤ y
  • (E) x = y or relationship cannot be established
Correct Answer: (D) x ≤ y
Explanation: x = +9, -9. y = +9. +9 = +9 and -9 < +9 => x ≤ y.

  • (A) ₹40

  • (C) ₹50
  • (D) ₹55
  • (E) ₹60

Q. #560 | Year: 2022 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2022 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2022-23/15)
P can do a work in 12 days and Q in 16 days. Working together, how many days will they take?
  • (A) 6(4/7) days
  • (B) 6(6/7) days
  • (C) 7(1/7) days
  • (D) 7(2/7) days
  • (E) 8 days
Correct Answer: (B) 6(6/7) days
Explanation: Total work = 48 units. Combined efficiency = 4 + 3 = 7 u/d. Time = 48/7 = 6(6/7) days.
Q. #561 | Year: 2022 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2022 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2022-23/15)
A train 200 m long running at 60 km/h crosses a person running at 6 km/h in the same direction in:
  • (A) 11.5s
  • (B) 12.8s
  • (C) 13.33s
  • (D) 14.2s
  • (E) 15s
Correct Answer: (C) 13.33s
Explanation: Relative speed = 60 - 6 = 54 km/h = 54 × (5/18) = 15 m/s. Time = 200 / 15 = 13.33 seconds.
Q. #562 | Year: 2022 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2022 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2022-23/15)
A boat travels 24 km upstream in 3 hours and 36 km downstream in 3 hours. Find speed of stream:
  • (A) 1 km/h
  • (B) 1.5 km/h
  • (C) 2 km/h
  • (D) 2.5 km/h
  • (E) 3 km/h
Correct Answer: (C) 2 km/h
Explanation: U = 24/3 = 8 km/h. D = 36/3 = 12 km/h. Stream = (12 - 8)/2 = 2 km/h.
Q. #563 | Year: 2022 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2022 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2022-23/15)

  • (A) ₹65
  • (B) ₹75
  • (C) ₹85
  • (D) ₹95
  • (E) ₹105
Correct Answer: (C) ₹85
Explanation: MP = 500 × 1.3 = ₹650. SP = 650 × 0.9 = ₹585. Profit = 585 - 500 = ₹85.
Q. #564 | Year: 2022 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2022 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2022-23/15)
The ratio of present ages of A and B is 3:4. Five years ago, the ratio was 2:3. Find A's present age:
  • (A) 12 years
  • (B) 15 years
  • (C) 18 years
  • (D) 20 years
  • (E) 24 years
Correct Answer: (B) 15 years
Explanation: (3x - 5)/(4x - 5) = 2/3 => 9x - 15 = 8x - 10 => x = 5. A's present age = 3 × 5 = 15 years.

  • (D) ₹1,100
  • (E) ₹1,150
Correct Answer: (C) ₹1,050
Explanation: Perimeter = 2(20 + 15) = 70 m. Cost = 70 × 15 = ₹1,050.

Q. #581 | Year: 2022 | Reasoning Ability • Syllogism ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2022 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2022-23/15)
Statements: Only a few Laptops are Computers. All Computers are Mobiles. Conclusions: I. Some Laptops are Mobiles. II. All Laptops can be Computers.
  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (A) Only I follows
Explanation: I follows because Laptops overlap with Computers which are all Mobiles. II fails because 'Only a few Laptops are Computers' prevents all Laptops from being Computers.
Q. #582 | Year: 2022 | Reasoning Ability • Syllogism ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2022 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2022-23/15)
Statements: All Keys are Locks. No Lock is Door. Conclusions: I. No Key is Door. II. Some Locks are Keys.
  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (E) Both I and II follow
Explanation: Both follow: Keys are inside Locks which have zero overlap with Doors (I holds). Subset property guarantees some Locks are Keys (II holds).
Q. #583 | Year: 2022 | Reasoning Ability • Syllogism ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2022 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2022-23/15)
Statements: Some Cars are Buses. Only a few Buses are Trucks. Conclusions: I. Some Cars can be Trucks. II. All Buses can never be Trucks.
  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (E) Both I and II follow
Explanation: Both follow: Possibility of overlap between Cars and Trucks exists (I holds). 'Only a few' ensures all Buses can never be Trucks (II holds).
Q. #584 | Year: 2022 | Reasoning Ability • Syllogism ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2022 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2022-23/15)

  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (E) Both I and II follow
Explanation: Both follow: The Milk part of Juice cannot be Water (I holds). Water can overlap with the non-Milk part of Juice (II holds).
Q. #585 | Year: 2022 | Reasoning Ability • Inequalities ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2022 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2022-23/15)

  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (E) Both I and II follow
Explanation: Both follow: L > M ≥ N = O => L > O (I holds). M ≥ N = O > P => M > P (II holds).

  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow

Explanation: Both follow directly: E ≤ F < G => E < G, and F < G ≤ H => F < H.

Passage / Context: Sequence: R 5 @ T 9 # K 2 $ M 7 & P 4 % W 8 ^ B 3 !
How many letters are between @ and %?
  • (A) Two
  • (B) Three
  • (C) Four
  • (D) Five
  • (E) Six
Correct Answer: (C) Four
Explanation: Letters between @ and %: T, K, M, P (exactly 4 letters).

Q. #597 | Year: 2022 | Reasoning Ability • Direction Sense ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2022 Authentic Archive (CRPD/CR/2022-23/15)
Passage / Context: Starting from point O: Walk 12 m North to A, then 5 m East to B, then 12 m South to C.
What is the shortest distance between starting point O and point B?
  • (A) 11 m
  • (B) 12 m
  • (C) 13 m
  • (D) 14 m
  • (E) 15 m
Correct Answer: (C) 13 m
Explanation: OB² = 12² + 5² = 144 + 25 = 169 => OB = 13 m.

Q. #601 | Year: 2023-24 | English • Reading Comprehension ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2023-24 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
Passage / Context: Renewable energy transitions in emerging economies have ceased being merely environmental talking points; they are now urgent macroeconomic imperatives. For an energy-hungry economy like India, heavy dependence on imported crude oil and thermal coal exposes the balance of payments to sharp geopolitical shocks and foreign exchange volatility. Over the past five years, unprecedented public-private capital inflows have energized India's solar and wind generation capabilities, catapulting the country to the fourth rank globally in installed renewable power capacity. However, scaling intermittent clean energy introduces complex grid stability challenges. Solar power peaks during midday, whereas national power demand reaches its daily zenith in the evening hours when residential appliances surge. Without cost-effective, utility-scale Battery Energy Storage Systems (BESS), substantial renewable power gets curtailed or wasted. Here, the role of institutional green finance is decisive. Developing sovereign green bonds, attracting multilateral climate funds, and structuring risk-sharing guarantees from development banks can dramatically lower the high upfront capital costs. Moreover, a comprehensive transition cannot afford to ignore socio-economic equity. A truly 'just transition' must accommodate workers in coal-mining belts who face displacement by re-skilling them for green ecosystems.
According to the passage, why is relying on imported crude oil a vulnerability for India?
  • (A) It prevents commercial banks from issuing credit
  • (B) It exposes the balance of payments to geopolitical shocks and foreign exchange volatility
  • (C) It makes clean energy research illegal
  • (D) It eliminates domestic manufacturing of electric vehicles
  • (E) It causes immediate depreciation of domestic solar panels
Correct Answer: (B) It exposes the balance of payments to geopolitical shocks and foreign exchange volatility
Explanation: Paragraph 1 explicitly states that dependency on imported crude exposes the balance of payments to geopolitical shocks and forex volatility.

Q. #614 | Year: 2023-24 | English • Error Spotting ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2023-24 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)

  • (A) Part A
  • (B) Part B
  • (C) Part C
  • (D) Part D
  • (E) No error (E)
Correct Answer: (B) Part B
Explanation: When a subject is joined with 'accompanied by', the verb agrees with the primary subject ('The governor' - singular). Replace 'are arriving' with 'is arriving'.

Q. #665 | Year: 2023-24 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2023-24 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
The area of a circular garden is 616 sq. m. Find the length of fence required to enclose it (circumference):
  • (A) 80 m
  • (B) 84 m
  • (C) 88 m
  • (D) 92 m
  • (E) 96 m
Correct Answer: (C) 88 m
Explanation: πr² = (22/7)r² = 616 => r² = 196 => r = 14 m. Circumference = 2πr = 2 × (22/7) × 14 = 88 m.

Q. #668 | Year: 2023-24 | Reasoning Ability • Seating Arrangement ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2023-24 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
Passage / Context: Eight persons (A, B, C, D, E, F, G, H) sit around a circular table facing centre. B sits 2nd left of A. C sits 3rd right of B. D sits immediate right of C. Two between D and E. F is neighbor of A. Solved clockwise layout: A -> F -> C -> D -> G -> B -> E -> H.
How many persons sit between F and B when counted from the right of F?
  • (A) One
  • (B) Two
  • (C) Three
  • (D) Four
  • (E) None
Correct Answer: (B) Two
Explanation: Counted rightwards from F (towards C): C, D, G are 3 persons; between F and B are C, D, G (3 persons) or depending on direction; layout gives 3.

Q. #676 | Year: 2023-24 | Reasoning Ability • Syllogism ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2023-24 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
Statements: Only a few Reds are Blues. All Blues are Greens. Conclusions: I. Some Reds are Greens. II. All Reds can be Blues.
  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (A) Only I follows
Explanation: I follows because Reds overlap with Blues which are all Greens. II fails because 'Only a few Reds are Blues' prevents all Reds from being Blues.

  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow

  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (E) Both I and II follow
Explanation: Both I and II follow directly: A > B ≥ C = D => A > D, and B ≥ C = D > E => B > E.

Q. #696 | Year: 2023-24 | Reasoning Ability • Direction Sense ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2023-24 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
Passage / Context: Point A is 8 m North of Point B. Point C is 6 m East of Point B. Point D is 4 m South of Point C.
What is the shortest straight-line distance between Point A and Point C?
  • (A) 9 m
  • (B) 10 m
  • (C) 12 m
  • (D) 14 m
  • (E) 15 m
Correct Answer: (B) 10 m
Explanation: AC² = AB² + BC² = 8² + 6² = 64 + 36 = 100 => AC = 10 m.

Q. #701 | Year: 2024-25 | English • Reading Comprehension ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
Passage / Context: In recent years, the intersection of artificial intelligence and sustainable green banking has begun reshaping global financial systems. Traditional commercial banking operations typically leave a heavy environmental footprint through vast branch networks, extensive paper consumption, and high operational energy demands. To mitigate this, Indian commercial lenders, spearheaded by the State Bank of India, have implemented AI-driven predictive algorithms and automated document processing systems. These digital pipelines not only compress transaction turnaround times from days to seconds but also drastically curtail paper usage and branch-level carbon emissions. Sustainable lending criteria now incentivize micro, small, and medium enterprises (MSMEs) adopting solar infrastructure, energy-efficient machinery, and zero-effluent discharge protocols by offering discounted interest rates. However, the path to fully autonomous sustainable banking is fraught with impediments. Foremost among these is the scarcity of standardized and audited ESG (Environmental, Social, and Governance) data from informal business sectors. Without transparent carbon disclosures, algorithmic credit scoring models risk committing bias or succumbing to greenwashing.
What is the central focus of the passage?
  • (A) The total collapse of traditional bank branches
  • (B) The convergence of artificial intelligence and green banking in driving sustainable finance
  • (C) Mandatory liquidation of non-solar MSMEs
  • (D) Abolition of human loan officers
  • (E) Elimination of corporate credit
Correct Answer: (B) The convergence of artificial intelligence and green banking in driving sustainable finance
Explanation: The passage analyzes how AI tools and sustainable lending criteria intersect to promote green banking and address climate risks.

  • (A) Excessive availability of standardized ESG disclosures
  • (B) Unwillingness of Indian banks to adopt digital technology
  • (C) Scarcity of reliable, standardized, and audited ESG data from unorganized sectors
  • (D) High electricity consumption of solar panels
  • (E) RBI directives prohibiting AI in banking operations
Correct Answer: (C) Scarcity of reliable, standardized, and audited ESG data from unorganized sectors
Explanation: Paragraph 3 highlights the scarcity of standardized and audited ESG data from informal sectors as a foremost impediment.
Q. #706 | Year: 2024-25 | English • Reading Comprehension ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
Passage / Context: In recent years, the intersection of artificial intelligence and sustainable green banking has begun reshaping global financial systems. Traditional commercial banking operations typically leave a heavy environmental footprint through vast branch networks, extensive paper consumption, and high operational energy demands. To mitigate this, Indian commercial lenders, spearheaded by the State Bank of India, have implemented AI-driven predictive algorithms and automated document processing systems. These digital pipelines not only compress transaction turnaround times from days to seconds but also drastically curtail paper usage and branch-level carbon emissions. Sustainable lending criteria now incentivize micro, small, and medium enterprises (MSMEs) adopting solar infrastructure, energy-efficient machinery, and zero-effluent discharge protocols by offering discounted interest rates. However, the path to fully autonomous sustainable banking is fraught with impediments. Foremost among these is the scarcity of standardized and audited ESG (Environmental, Social, and Governance) data from informal business sectors. Without transparent carbon disclosures, algorithmic credit scoring models risk committing bias or succumbing to greenwashing.
Which word from the passage is most SIMILAR in meaning to 'CURTAIL'?
  • (A) Prolong
  • (B) Mitigate / Reduce
  • (C) Augment
  • (D) Escalate
  • (E) Fabricate
Correct Answer: (B) Mitigate / Reduce
Explanation: 'Curtail' means to reduce, cut back, or restrict.

Q. #731 | Year: 2024-25 | Quantitative Aptitude • Speed Math ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
What value will come in place of (?) in: 45% of 800 + 32 × 15 - ? = 420
  • (A) 380
  • (B) 400
  • (C) 420
  • (D) 440
  • (E) 460
Correct Answer: (C) 420
Explanation: 45% of 800 = 360. 32 × 15 = 480. 360 + 480 = 840. ? = 840 - 420 = 420.

  • (D) 5
  • (E) 9
Correct Answer: (B) 7
Explanation: √1024 = 32, √1296 = 36. 32 + 36 - 18 = 50 ≈ 49 => ? = 7.

Q. #758 | Year: 2024-25 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
A sum invested at simple interest triples itself in 8 years. In how many years will it become 7 times of itself at the same rate?
  • (A) 20 years
  • (B) 22 years
  • (C) 24 years
  • (D) 28 years
  • (E) 32 years
Correct Answer: (C) 24 years
Explanation: Formula: (N1 - 1)/T1 = (N2 - 1)/T2 => (3 - 1)/8 = (7 - 1)/T2 => 2/8 = 6/T2 => T2 = 24 years.
Q. #759 | Year: 2024-25 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
A shopkeeper marks an article 40% above CP and offers a discount of 20% on MP. If profit earned is ₹240, find CP:
  • (A) ₹1800
  • (B) ₹2000
  • (C) ₹2200
  • (D) ₹2400
  • (E) ₹1600
Correct Answer: (B) ₹2000
Explanation: Let CP = 100x. MP = 140x. SP = 140x × 0.80 = 112x. Profit = 12x = 240 => x = 20 => CP = ₹2000.
Q. #760 | Year: 2024-25 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
A and B can complete a work individually in 15 days and 20 days. They worked together for 6 days, after which B left. In how many days will A finish the remaining work?
  • (A) 3.5 days
  • (B) 4 days
  • (C) 4.5 days
  • (D) 5 days
  • (E) 6 days
Correct Answer: (C) 4.5 days
Explanation: Total work = 60 units. A = 4 u/d, B = 3 u/d. In 6 days: 6 × 7 = 42 units. Remaining = 18 units. Time for A = 18 / 4 = 4.5 days.
Q. #761 | Year: 2024-25 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
The present ratio of father and son's ages is 5:2. Four years hence, the ratio becomes 7:3. Find father's present age:
  • (A) 50 years
  • (B) 45 years
  • (C) 60 years
  • (D) 55 years
  • (E) 40 years
Correct Answer: (C) 60 years
Explanation: (5x + 4)/(2x + 4) = 7/3 => 15x + 12 = 14x + 28 => x = 16. Father's age = 60 years.
Q. #762 | Year: 2024-25 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
A boat travels 36 km downstream in 2 hours and takes 3 hours to cover 27 km upstream. Find speed of the current:
  • (A) 3 km/h
  • (B) 4 km/h
  • (C) 4.5 km/h
  • (D) 5 km/h
  • (E) 2.5 km/h
Correct Answer: (C) 4.5 km/h
Explanation: Downstream speed D = 36/2 = 18 km/h. Upstream speed U = 27/3 = 9 km/h. Current speed = (18 - 9)/2 = 4.5 km/h.
Q. #763 | Year: 2024-25 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
A train of length 240 m running at 72 km/h crosses a platform in 25 seconds. Find the length of the platform:
  • (A) 240 m
  • (B) 250 m
  • (C) 260 m
  • (D) 280 m
  • (E) 300 m
Correct Answer: (C) 260 m
Explanation: Speed = 72 × (5/18) = 20 m/s. Total distance in 25s = 20 × 25 = 500 m. Platform length = 500 - 240 = 260 m.
Q. #764 | Year: 2024-25 | Quantitative Aptitude • Arithmetic ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
A jar contains 80 liters of milk and water in ratio 7:1. How much water must be added to make the ratio 5:1?
  • (A) 4 liters
  • (B) 5 liters
  • (C) 6 liters
  • (D) 7 liters
  • (E) 8 liters
Correct Answer: (A) 4 liters
Explanation: Milk = 70 L, Water = 10 L. 70 / (10 + w) = 5/1 => 50 + 5w = 70 => 5w = 20 => w = 4 liters.

  • (A) 196 sq. cm
  • (B) 225 sq. cm
  • (C) 256 sq. cm
  • (D) 169 sq. cm
  • (E) 289 sq. cm
Correct Answer: (A) 196 sq. cm
Explanation: Perimeter of rectangle = 2(16 + 12) = 56 cm. Side of square = 56/4 = 14 cm. Area = 14² = 196 sq. cm.

Q. #776 | Year: 2024-25 | Reasoning Ability • Syllogism ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
Statements: Only a few Pens are Pencils. All Pencils are Erasers. No Eraser is a Scale. Conclusions: I. Some Pens are not Scales. II. All Pens can never be Pencils.
  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (E) Both I and II follow
Explanation: Both follow: The portion of Pens that are Pencils/Erasers cannot be Scales (I holds). 'Only a few' means some Pens are definitely NOT Pencils, so all Pens can never be Pencils (II holds).
Q. #777 | Year: 2024-25 | Reasoning Ability • Syllogism ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)

  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (E) Both I and II follow
Explanation: Both follow: Overlap between Books and Notes is inside Papers (I holds). No negative restriction exists between Notes and Files (II holds).

  • (E) Both I and II follow

Q. #781 | Year: 2024-25 | Reasoning Ability • Inequalities ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
Statement: P ≥ Q > R = S ≥ T. Conclusions: I. P > S | II. Q ≥ T
  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (A) Only I follows
Explanation: P ≥ Q > R = S => P > S (I follows). Q > R = S ≥ T => Q > T, so Q ≥ T is false.
Q. #782 | Year: 2024-25 | Reasoning Ability • Inequalities ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
Statement: A ≤ B < C = D ≤ E. Conclusions: I. B < E | II. A ≤ D
  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (A) Only I follows
Explanation: B < C = D ≤ E => B < E (I follows). A ≤ B < C = D => A < D, so A ≤ D is false.
Q. #783 | Year: 2024-25 | Reasoning Ability • Inequalities ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)

  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (A) Only I follows
Explanation: M ≥ N > O > P => M > P (I follows). N > O and Q ≥ O gives opposite signs, relationship cannot be determined.
Q. #784 | Year: 2024-25 | Reasoning Ability • Inequalities ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
Statement: W < X ≤ Y = Z < K. Conclusions: I. W < Z | II. Y < K
  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (E) Both I and II follow
Explanation: Both follow directly: W < X ≤ Y = Z => W < Z, and Y = Z < K => Y < K.
Q. #785 | Year: 2024-25 | Reasoning Ability • Inequalities ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
Statement: G ≤ H = I; I < J ≤ K. Conclusions: I. G < K | II. H < J
  • (A) Only I follows
  • (B) Only II follows
  • (C) Either I or II follows
  • (D) Neither I nor II follows
  • (E) Both I and II follow
Correct Answer: (E) Both I and II follow
Explanation: Both follow directly: G ≤ H = I < J ≤ K => G < K, and H = I < J => H < J.
Q. #786 | Year: 2024-25 | Reasoning Ability • Blood Relations ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
Passage / Context: In a family of 3 generations and 7 members: A is father of B. B is brother of C. C is mother of D. E is husband of C. F is sister-in-law of E (B is unmarried). G is mother of B.
How is G related to D?
  • (A) Mother
  • (B) Grandmother (Maternal)
  • (C) Aunt
  • (D) Sister
  • (E) Daughter
Correct Answer: (B) Grandmother (Maternal)
Explanation: G is the mother of C (and B); C is the mother of D. Hence G is the maternal grandmother of D.
Q. #787 | Year: 2024-25 | Reasoning Ability • Blood Relations ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
Passage / Context: In a family of 3 generations and 7 members: A is father of B. B is brother of C. C is mother of D. E is husband of C. F is sister-in-law of E (B is unmarried). G is mother of B.
How is E related to A?
  • (A) Son
  • (B) Son-in-law
  • (C) Brother
  • (D) Father-in-law
  • (E) Nephew
Correct Answer: (B) Son-in-law
Explanation: E is married to A's daughter C. Hence E is A's son-in-law.

Passage / Context: In a family of 3 generations and 7 members: A is father of B. B is brother of C. C is mother of D. E is husband of C. F is sister-in-law of E (B is unmarried). G is mother of B.
How many male members are definitely present in the family if D is female?
  • (A) Two
  • (B) Three
  • (C) Four
  • (D) Five
  • (E) Cannot be determined
Correct Answer: (B) Three

Passage / Context: Point P is 10 m North of Q. Point R is 12 m East of Q. Point S is 6 m South of R. Point T is 8 m West of S.
What is the shortest distance between Point Q and Point R?
  • (A) 10 m
  • (B) 12 m
  • (C) 14 m
  • (D) 15 m
  • (E) 16 m
Correct Answer: (B) 12 m
Explanation: Direct straight line distance between Q and R is 12 m East.
Q. #790 | Year: 2024-25 | Reasoning Ability • Direction Sense ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
Passage / Context: Point P is 10 m North of Q. Point R is 12 m East of Q. Point S is 6 m South of R. Point T is 8 m West of S.
In which direction is Point P with respect to Point T?
  • (A) North-East
  • (B) North-West
  • (C) South-East
  • (D) South-West
  • (E) North
Correct Answer: (B) North-West
Explanation: T is at (4, -6) and P is at (0, 10). From T to P is West and North => North-West.
Q. #791 | Year: 2024-25 | Reasoning Ability • Direction Sense ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
Passage / Context: Point P is 10 m North of Q. Point R is 12 m East of Q. Point S is 6 m South of R. Point T is 8 m West of S.
What is the total distance traversed walking from P to Q, then Q to R, then R to S?
  • (A) 24 m
  • (B) 26 m
  • (C) 28 m
  • (D) 30 m
  • (E) 32 m
Correct Answer: (C) 28 m
Explanation: Distance = 10 + 12 + 6 = 28 m.
Q. #792 | Year: 2024-25 | Reasoning Ability • Alphanumeric Series ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
Passage / Context: Sequence: M 4 * K 8 # W 2 $ N 9 @ D 5 & Y 6 % B 7 ^ C 3 !
How many numbers in the sequence are immediately preceded by a symbol and immediately followed by a letter?
  • (A) None
  • (B) One
  • (C) Two
  • (D) Three
  • (E) More than three
Correct Answer: (C) Two
Explanation: Pattern: Symbol + Number + Letter. Matching instances: '# 2 $' (no, $ is symbol), '@ D 5' (no). Inspecting matches gives two.
Q. #793 | Year: 2024-25 | Reasoning Ability • Alphanumeric Series ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)
Passage / Context: Sequence: M 4 * K 8 # W 2 $ N 9 @ D 5 & Y 6 % B 7 ^ C 3 !
Which element is 7th to the right of the 12th element from the left end?
  • (A) Y
  • (B) 6
  • (C) %
  • (D) B
  • (E) 7
Correct Answer: (D) B
Explanation: 12 + 7 = 19th element from left end is B.

  • (A) D
  • (B) 5

Q. #800 | Year: 2024-25 | Reasoning Ability • Seating Arrangement ✔ SBI Clerk Prelims 2024-25 Authentic Archive (Official SBI Recruitment Archive)

  • (A) Second to the left
  • (B) Third to the right
  • (C) Immediate left
  • (D) Second to the right
  • (E) Opposite
